# Full benchmark command list

Generated by `python docs/make_run_commands.py` (do not edit by hand). The procedure and one-shot loops are in the root `README.md` ("Running the Full Benchmark") and `docs/RUNPOD.md`.

- Run every command from the **repo root** with the virtual environment active (`source .venv/bin/activate` on RunPod, `.venv\Scripts\activate` on Windows). Each command is a single line, so it works in bash and PowerShell.
- **Do not add hyperparameter flags** (`--learning_rate`, `--hid_dim`, ...): explicit flags override the paper / tuned values.
- Seeds: 123, 1234, 12345, 123456, 1234567. Training ratios: 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4 (valid 20% and test 40% stay fixed).
- Outputs: `results/<Model>/<dataset>/seed<seed>_train<pct>/`. The folder name is built from the arguments, so no two commands below share a folder and running the list from top to bottom never overwrites anything (`config.json` records `train_ratio` and `hparam_source`).
- Limits per run: more than 4 hours stops the run and writes `OOT.txt` (exit code 124); more than 100 GB of GPU memory stops it and writes `OOM.txt` (exit code 125), both in the run folder. Skip a command whose folder already has `metrics.json`, `OOT.txt` or `OOM.txt`.

Totals: 77 tuning commands (step 1), 2760 training commands (step 2), 23 models.

## Step 1 - Hyperparameter tuning (pairs not evaluated in the original paper)

Run once, before any training. Each command runs 20 trials x 5 seeds on the 40% split and writes `configs/tuned/<Model>/<dataset>.json`; add `--resume` to continue an interrupted pair. Equivalent single command: `python tune_hparams.py --all --gpu 0`.

### AMNet

```bash
python tune_hparams.py --model AMNet --dataset amazon --gpu 0
python tune_hparams.py --model AMNet --dataset tfinance --gpu 0
python tune_hparams.py --model AMNet --dataset tsocial --gpu 0
python tune_hparams.py --model AMNet --dataset comp --gpu 0
```
